Hiring Process / Applying Process Review Research Packet
50 companies x 10 applicant-facing review summaries
Prepared: June 30, 2026 | Scope: U.S. applicant/hiring-process signals
Important note: This document uses paraphrased public-review themes and applicant-facing process summaries. It does not copy 500 raw anonymous reviews verbatim. Public platforms such as Glassdoor, Indeed, Reddit, and company career pages often restrict full review access, require login, or mix job families and countries. To keep this usable and clean, each company receives ten review-style summaries that capture recurring candidate experiences, likely process stages, and SeenJobs-style signals to track.
Company selection: The 50-company base follows LinkedIn News' 2026 U.S. Top Companies list, then cross-checks the idea of applicant demand against other employer-recognition sources such as Handshake Early Talent Awards and Fortune/Great Place To Work. LinkedIn's list is useful here because it is built around career-growth signals, not just brand awareness.
How to use this: Treat each row as a seed datapoint for SeenJobs review cards: process stage, pain point, applicant signal, and source confidence. For production, replace each paraphrase with a verified user-submitted outcome, timestamp, job family, source URL, and screenshot/hash evidence when possible.
Source and confidence rules
- High confidence = the stage is widely documented for the role family/company category and is consistent with public candidate-review patterns.
- Medium confidence = credible public themes exist, but exact experience varies heavily by team, location, country, recruiter, or job family.
- Low confidence = public-access detail is thin; use the row as a hypothesis to verify with real applicant submissions before ranking a company.
- These are summaries, not direct quotes. Do not present them publicly as exact individual reviews without collecting/verifying the original review text and date.

2. Alphabet / Google
Common roles surfaced: Software Engineer, Product Manager, Account Executive | Typical flow: online application/referral -> recruiter screen -> technical/product assessment -> manager/team loop -> offer committee or team match | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect role-specific variance: engineering/product/corporate paths are materially different from sales, support, store, or operations paths.

3. Microsoft
Common roles surfaced: Software Engineer, Program Manager, Product Manager | Typical flow: online application/referral -> recruiter screen -> technical/product assessment -> manager/team loop -> offer committee or team match | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect role-specific variance: engineering/product/corporate paths are materially different from sales, support, store, or operations paths.

4. Amazon
Common roles surfaced: Software Engineer, Supply Chain Associate, Business Operations Manager | Typical flow: online application -> assessment/phone screen -> behavioral/technical rounds -> loop or store/team interview -> background check | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect role-specific variance: engineering/product/corporate paths are materially different from sales, support, store, or operations paths.

Best next real-data fields to collect for this company: role title, location, source channel, application date, first response date, interview count, assessment type, final outcome, ghost/reject/hired status, compensation visibility, and whether applicant had referral/internal contact.
9. AT&T
Common roles surfaced: Retail Sales Consultant, Software Engineer, Account Executive | Typical flow: application -> assessment or recruiter screen -> role-play/manager interview -> background check -> training | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect location-level urgency to matter; two applicants can see totally different speeds at different sites.

11. CVS Health
Common roles surfaced: Pharmacy Technician, Customer Service Representative, Business Operations Manager | Typical flow: online application -> screening questions -> manager/pharmacy interview -> background/drug/credential check -> onboarding | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect location-level urgency to matter; two applicants can see totally different speeds at different sites.

12. PwC
Common roles surfaced: Accounting Associate, Tax Accountant, Audit Associate | Typical flow: online application/referral -> HireVue or recruiter screen -> behavioral/case rounds -> senior/partner interview -> offer | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect structured rounds, cohort/project timing, and heavy emphasis on client-facing examples.

Best next real-data fields to collect for this company: role title, location, source channel, application date, first response date, interview count, assessment type, final outcome, ghost/reject/hired status, compensation visibility, and whether applicant had referral/internal contact.
20. Apple
Common roles surfaced: Software Engineer, Technical Support Representative, Sales Specialist | Typical flow: online application -> assessment/phone screen -> behavioral/technical rounds -> loop or store/team interview -> background check | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect role-specific variance: engineering/product/corporate paths are materially different from sales, support, store, or operations paths.

22. Fidelity Investments
Common roles surfaced: Customer Support Specialist, Software Engineer, Investment Representative | Typical flow: resume screen -> recruiter call -> behavioral/technical or business screen -> panel/superday -> background/compliance checks | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect a regulated process with structured behavioral questions and extra verification after offer.

36. Accenture
Common roles surfaced: Business Consultant, Managing Director, Software Engineer | Typical flow: online application -> recruiter screen -> technical/case discussion -> project/team interview -> offer tied to role fit | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect structured rounds, cohort/project timing, and heavy emphasis on client-facing examples.

44. Delta Air Lines
Common roles surfaced: Flight Attendant, Pilot, Customer Service Representative | Typical flow: online application -> assessment/video interview -> group or panel interview -> background/drug/fingerprint checks -> training | Source confidence baseline: Medium-High, unless a row notes otherwise.
Expect location-level urgency to matter; two applicants can see totally different speeds at different sites.

Expect site, shift, safety, technical tooling, and supervisor approval to control the process.

Appendix A - Research sources checked
Core source set used to select companies and frame the process signals. Some platforms expose only snippets or require sign-in for full review detail. These links are included so the document can be audited and expanded with direct screenshots/exports later.
[1] LinkedIn News - Top Companies 2026: The 50 best employers to grow your career in the U.S.: https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/linkedin-top-companies-2026-50-best-large-employers-grow-zupfe
[2] Handshake - 2026 Early Talent Awards: https://joinhandshake.com/early-talent-awards/
[3] Great Place To Work / Fortune - 100 Best Companies to Work For 2026: https://www.greatplacetowork.com/best-workplaces/100-best/2026
[4] Glassdoor - Interview Questions and Reviews index: https://www.glassdoor.com/Interview/index.htm
[5] Glassdoor - Candidate experience guide: https://www.glassdoor.com/blog/a-guide-to-the-ultimate-candidate-experience/
[6] Indeed - Smart Screening overview: https://www.indeed.com/hire/resources/howtohub/what-is-indeed-smart-screening
[7] Indeed - Employer tools/hiring guide: https://www.indeed.com/hire/resources/howtohub/hiring-guide-using-employer-tools
[8] TIME - Why finding a job is difficult: https://time.com/6287012/why-finding-job-is-difficult/
[9] Washington Post - Employers ghosting applicants: https://www.washingtonpost.com/business/2025/09/19/job-seekers-employers-ghosting/
Suggested company-level verification workflow: For each company, search public interview-review pages by company + job title + location; record the review date, application channel, outcome, process length, interview count, and exact stage sequence. For Reddit or forum data, keep permalink, subreddit, post/comment date, role/location if present, and avoid reposting long verbatim text publicly.
